2417 — resistance band duck walk

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2417 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A low squat walk with a band around the legs that fires up the glutes and hips.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

authored_from_source_context; source verified: false. Body-pose class uses cited setup/action or title/description context, or an explicitly authored neutral default; detailed placement is a reversible proposal. Basis: source_classification. 2417.json#/name

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 2417.json#/name

Legs Feet

Place a loop band around your thighs or knees.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2417.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Resistance loop band, Resistance band base family, Band anchor point. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2417.json#/name

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Place a loop band around your thighs or knees. 2417.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lower into a half squat with your chest up. 2417.json#/instructions/1
  3. Step sideways or forward with a wide stride. 2417.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep tension on the band and your knees out. 2417.json#/instructions/3
  5. Bring your other foot in, staying low. 2417.json#/instructions/4
  6. Walk for the planned distance, then return. 2417.json#/instructions/5

2418 — resistance band bent leg side kick (kneeling)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2418 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: quadruped.

A kneeling side kick with a band that targets the glutes and outer hips.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Kneel on all fours with a band around your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2418.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Place open palms on the support under the shoulders, with relaxed forward-facing fingers; this exact placement is an authored staging choice.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 2418.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Place the knees under the hips on the documented support and let the lower legs rest comfortably; preserve any source-defined lifted limb.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 2418.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Resistance loop band, Resistance band base family. For this floor-support pose, stage the documented hand, forearm, foot or knee supports on a level ground plane unless the source names another surface; this is an authored support choice. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2418.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Kneel on all fours with a band around your thighs. 2418.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep your core braced and your back flat. 2418.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lift one bent leg out to the side against the band. 2418.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause when your thigh is about hip height. 2418.json#/instructions/3
  5. Lower slowly without twisting your torso. 2418.json#/instructions/4
  6. Finish your reps, then switch legs. 2418.json#/instructions/5

2736 — lever reverse grip lateral pulldown

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2736 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A reverse grip lat pulldown on a machine. Builds the lats and biceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Adjust the seat height and position yourself on the machine with your knees under the pads and your feet flat on the ground. Grasp the handles with an overhand grip, slightly wider than shoulder-width apart. Sit upright with your chest out and shoulders back, maintaining a slight arch in your lower back.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Adjust the seat height and position yourself on the machine with your knees under the pads and your feet flat on the ground. 2736.json#/instructions/0
  2. Grasp the handles with an overhand grip, slightly wider than shoulder-width apart. 2736.json#/instructions/1
  3. Sit upright with your chest out and shoulders back, maintaining a slight arch in your lower back. 2736.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pull the handles down towards your chest, leading with your elbows and squeezing your shoulder blades together. 2736.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the bottom of the movement, then slowly release the handles back to the starting position. 2736.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 2736.json#/instructions/5
